    from __future__ import annotations
    
    import argparse
    import configparser
    import itertools
    import json
    import re
    import xml.etree.ElementTree as ET
    import zipfile
    from pathlib import Path
    
    # sentinel-ctl: policy bundle compiler.
    
    SELECTABLE_STATUS = ("active", "reinstated")
    
    
    def load_json(path):
        return json.loads(Path(path).read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
    
    
    def write_json(path, data):
        Path(path).parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
        text = json.dumps(data, sort_keys=True, indent=2) + "\n"
        Path(path).write_text(text, encoding="utf-8")
    
    
    def coerce(value):
        if re.fullmatch(r"-?[0-9]+", value):
            return int(value)
        if value in ("true", "false"):
            return value == "true"
        return value
    
    
    def assert_flat(data):
        bad = [key for key, value in data.items() if isinstance(value, (dict, 
list))]
        if bad:
            raise SystemExit("configuration must be flat: " + 
",".join(sorted(bad)))
    
    
    def read_toml(path):
        import tomllib
    
        return tomllib.loads(Path(path).read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
    
    
    def read_jsonc(path):
        text = Path(path).read_text(encoding="utf-8")
        text = re.sub(r"/\*.*?\*/", "", text, flags=re.S)
        text = re.sub(r"(^|\s)//[^\n]*", "", text)
        return json.loads(text)
    
    
    def read_yaml_flat(path):
        out = {}
        for line in Path(path).read_text(encoding="utf-8").splitlines():
            stripped = line.strip()
            if not stripped or stripped.startswith("#"):
                continue
            if ": " not in stripped and not stripped.endswith(":"):
                continue
            key, _, value = stripped.partition(":")
            out[key.strip()] = value.strip().strip('"')
        return out
    
    
    def read_kv_text(path):
        out = {}
        for line in Path(path).read_text(encoding="utf-8").splitlines():
            stripped = line.strip()
            if not stripped or stripped.startswith("#") or 
stripped.startswith(";"):
                continue
            key, sep, value = stripped.partition("=")
            if not sep:
                continue
            out[key.strip()] = value.strip()
        return out
    
    
    def _parser(path):
        parser = configparser.ConfigParser()
        parser.read(path, encoding="utf-8")
        return parser
    
    
    def read_conf(path):
        parser = _parser(path)
        flat = {}
        for section in parser.sections():
            for key, value in parser.items(section):
                flat[key] = value
        return flat
    
    
    def read_conf_section(path, section):
        parser = _parser(path)
        if not parser.has_section(section):
            return {}
        return dict(parser.items(section))
    
    
    def read_xml_records(path, tag):
        root = ET.parse(path).getroot()
        return [dict(node.attrib) for node in root.iter(tag)]
    
    
    def _col_to_idx(ref):
        letters = re.match(r"[A-Z]+", ref).group(0)
        idx = 0
        for char in letters:
            idx = idx * 26 + (ord(char) - ord("A") + 1)
        return idx - 1
    
    
    def read_xlsx_rows(path):
        ns = "{" + "http" + 
"://schemas.openxmlformats.org/spreadsheetml/2006/main}"
        with zipfile.ZipFile(path) as zf:
            sroot = ET.fromstring(zf.read("xl/worksheets/sheet1.xml"))
            rows = []
            for row in sroot.iter(ns + "row"):
                cells = {}
                for cell in row.iter(ns + "c"):
                    ref = cell.get("r", "A1")
                    col = _col_to_idx(ref)
                    vnode = cell.find(ns + "v")
                    if cell.get("t") == "inlineStr":
                        tnode = cell.find(ns + "is/" + ns + "t")
                        cells[col] = tnode.text if tnode is not None else ""
                    else:
                        cells[col] = vnode.text if vnode is not None else ""
                rows.append(cells)
        header = [rows[0][i] for i in sorted(rows[0])]
        records = []
        for cells in rows[1:]:
            records.append({name: cells.get(i, "") for i, name in 
enumerate(header)})
        return records
    
    
    def read_parquet_rows(path):
        import pyarrow.parquet as pq
    
        return pq.read_table(path).to_pylist()
    
    
    def round_half_up_bps(value, bps):
        """round_half_up(value * bps / 1000) in exact integer arithmetic."""
        return (value * bps + 500) // 1000
    
    
    def _run_curve(base_threshold, contribution, divisor, epsilon, 
max_iterations):
        iterations = [base_threshold]
        current = base_threshold
        for _ in range(max_iterations):
            nxt = base_threshold + (contribution + current) // divisor
            iterations.append(nxt)
            if abs(nxt - current) <= epsilon:
                return iterations, nxt
            current = nxt
        return iterations, None
    
    
    def _best_repair(spares, need_rows, need_mr, need_ms):
        """Smallest total absolute timeout, ties to the first ascending seq 
list."""
        if need_rows < 0 or need_rows > len(spares):
            return None
        rows = sorted(spares, key=lambda r: r["seq"])
        best = None
        for combo in itertools.combinations(rows, need_rows):
            if sum(r["mr"] for r in combo) != need_mr:
                continue
            if sum(r["ms"] for r in combo) != need_ms:
                continue
            seqs = [r["seq"] for r in combo]
            key = (sum(abs(r["ms"]) for r in combo), seqs)
            if best is None or key < best[0]:
                best = (key, seqs)
        return best[1] if best else None
    
    
    class Corpus:
        """The reference material, recording every file in the order it is 
consumed."""
    
        def __init__(self, data_dir):
            self.dir = Path(data_dir)
            self.order = []
    
        def path(self, name):
            if name not in self.order:
                self.order.append(name)
            return self.dir / name
    
        def text(self, name):
            return Path(self.path(name)).read_text(encoding="utf-8")
    
        def has(self, name):
            return (self.dir / name).exists()
    
    
    def resolve_policy(data_dir):
        cx = Corpus(data_dir)
    
        # boot manifest -> stale version rules -> ledger -> principal index -> 
overrides
        manifest = read_toml(cx.path("boot_manifest.toml"))["manifest"]
        instant = int(manifest["effective_instant"])
        stale = read_kv_text(cx.path("stale_version_rules.txt"))
        active_bundle = str(manifest[stale["prefer"]])
        ignored_bundle = str(manifest[stale["ignore"]])
    
        ledger = read_jsonc(cx.path(manifest["ledger_ref"]))
        bundle = ledger[active_bundle]
        role_key = bundle["role_key"]
        index = read_yaml_flat(cx.path(bundle["index_file"]))
    
        def role_params(key):
            return (
                index[key + ".param_key"],
                index[key + ".tenant"],
                int(index[key + ".role_level"]),
            )
    
        param_key, tenant, role_level = role_params(role_key)
    
        vmap = load_json(cx.path("policy_version_map.json"))
        version_rows = vmap["versions"]
        version = max(
            (v for v in version_rows if int(v["effective_from"]) <= instant),
            key=lambda v: int(v["effective_from"]),
        )["version"]
        effective_from = {v["version"]: int(v["effective_from"]) for v in 
version_rows}
    
        corpus_doc = load_json(cx.path("request_corpus.json"))
        client_version_ignored = any(
            entry["claimed_version"] != version for entry in 
corpus_doc["entries"]
        )
    
        units_text = cx.text("units_reference.txt")
        cutover = int(re.search(r"cutover:\s*(-?\d+)", units_text).group(1))
        ms_factor = int(re.search(r"ms\s*=\s*seconds\s*\*\s*(\d+)", 
units_text).group(1))
        timeout_floor = int(re.search(r"raised to (\d+)", units_text).group(1))
    
        base_rows = {
            row["param_key"]: row
            for row in read_parquet_rows(cx.path("base_parameters.parquet"))
        }
    
        odoc = load_json(cx.path(index["overrides_ref"]))
        stored = sorted(odoc["overrides"], key=lambda r: int(r["seq"]))
        alias_map = {a["alias"]: a for a in odoc.get("param_key_aliases", [])}
        reports = odoc.get("reports", [])
        controls = odoc.get("controls", {})
    
        windows = {
            w["id"]: w for w in 
read_xml_records(cx.path("effective_windows.xml"), "window")
        }
        read_conf(cx.path("override_precedence.conf"))
        catalog = {
            t["id"]: t for t in read_xml_records(cx.path("tenant_catalog.xml"), 
"tenant")
        }
        allowance = {
            key: int(value)
            for key, value in read_conf_section(
                cx.path("quota_limits.cfg"), "override_allowance"
            ).items()
        }
    
        def principal_of(row):
            entry = alias_map.get(row["param_key"])
            if entry is None:
                return row["param_key"]
            if int(entry["start"]) <= instant < int(entry["end"]):
                return entry["principal"]
            return None
    
        def delta_ms(row):
            value = int(row["timeout_ms_delta"])
            return value * ms_factor if effective_from[row["version"]] < cutover
else value
    
        latest_seq = {}
        for row in stored:
            latest_seq[row["override_id"]] = int(row["seq"])
    
        def source_row(row):
            """A reinstated row adds the deltas of the last active row below its
seq."""
            if row["status"] != "reinstated":
                return row
            prior = [
                r
                for r in stored
                if r["override_id"] == row["override_id"]
                and r["status"] == "active"
                and int(r["seq"]) < int(row["seq"])
            ]
            return prior[-1] if prior else None
    
        # selection per override_precedence.conf
        refused = []
        passing = []
        for row in stored:
            window = windows.get(row["window_id"])
            if int(row["seq"]) != latest_seq[row["override_id"]]:
                reason = "reissued: the same override_id is stored at a higher 
seq"
            elif row["status"] not in SELECTABLE_STATUS:
                reason = "status " + row["status"] + " passes no selection"
            elif window is None:
                reason = "window " + row["window_id"] + " is not in 
effective_windows.xml"
            elif not int(window["start"]) <= instant < int(window["end"]):
                reason = (
                    "window " + row["window_id"]
                    + " does not contain effective_instant in its half-open 
span"
                )
            elif row["version"] != version:
                reason = (
                    "row version " + row["version"]
                    + " is not the effective version " + version
                )
            elif window["version"] != version:
                reason = (
                    "window " + row["window_id"] + " carries version " + 
window["version"]
                    + ", not the effective version " + version
                )
            elif source_row(row) is None:
                reason = "reinstated with no earlier active row of its 
override_id"
            else:
                reason = None
            (passing if reason is None else refused).append(
                row if reason is None else (row, reason)
            )
    
        # draw: ascending seq, one region allowance unit and one window admit 
unit each
        admit_left = {wid: int(w.get("admit", 0)) for wid, w in windows.items()}
        allow_left = dict(allowance)
        applied = []
        applied_ids = set()
        for row in passing:
            region = catalog[row["tenant"]]["region"]
            if "requires" in row and row["requires"] not in applied_ids:
                refused.append(
                    (
                        row,
                        "requires " + str(row["requires"])
                        + ", which was not applied at a lower seq",
                    )
                )
                continue
            if allow_left.get(region, 0) <= 0:
                refused.append(
                    (row, "region " + region + " has no override_allowance unit 
left")
                )
                continue
            if admit_left.get(row["window_id"], 0) <= 0:
                refused.append((row, "window " + row["window_id"] + " has no 
admit unit left"))
                continue
            allow_left[region] -= 1
            admit_left[row["window_id"]] -= 1
            applied.append(row)
            applied_ids.add(row["override_id"])
    
        # the applied rows of a window share that window's timeout_pool
        added_ms = {}
        for wid in sorted({row["window_id"] for row in applied}):
            pool = int(windows[wid].get("timeout_pool", 0))
            positive = []
            for row in applied:
                if row["window_id"] != wid:
                    continue
                value = delta_ms(source_row(row))
                if value > 0:
                    positive.append((int(row["seq"]), value))
                else:
                    added_ms[int(row["seq"])] = value
            total = sum(value for _, value in positive)
            if total <= pool:
                for seq, value in positive:
                    added_ms[seq] = value
                continue
            shares = {seq: pool * value // total for seq, value in positive}
            keep = [(seq, value) for seq, value in positive if shares[seq] >= 
100]
            kept = sum(value for _, value in keep)
            for seq, _ in positive:
                added_ms[seq] = 0
            for seq, value in keep:
                added_ms[seq] = pool * value // kept
    
        # a superseded row with a carry field contributes that one delta
        carried = []
        for row in stored:
            window = windows.get(row["window_id"])
            if row["status"] != "superseded" or "carry" not in row or window is 
None:
                continue
            if not int(window["start"]) <= instant < int(window["end"]):
                continue
            if row["version"] == version and window["version"] == version:
                carried.append(row)
    
        multipliers = {
            row["tenant"]: row
            for row in read_xlsx_rows(cx.path("tenant_multiplier_table.xlsx"))
        }
        cx.text("clamp_rules.txt")
        cx.text("risk_weight_rounding.txt")
        tier_curve = {
            int(row["tier"]): int(row["base_threshold"])
            for row in 
read_parquet_rows(cx.path("threshold_tier_curve.parquet"))
        }
    
        conv_text = cx.text("convergence_spec.txt")
        epsilon = int(re.search(r"convergence_epsilon\s*=\s*(-?\d+)", 
conv_text).group(1))
        max_iterations = int(re.search(r"max_iterations\s*=\s*(\d+)", 
conv_text).group(1))
    
        rec_text = cx.text("reconciliation_rules.conf")
        rec = read_conf(cx.path("reconciliation_rules.conf"))
        bounds = re.search(r"whole number from (\d+) to (\d+)", rec_text)
        divisor_range = range(int(bounds.group(1)), int(bounds.group(2)) + 1)
    
        def compile_params(key, tenant_id, level):
            base = base_rows[key]
            mult = multipliers[tenant_id]
            mult_bps = int(mult["mult_bps"])
            cap = int(mult["max_records_cap"])
            mine = [row for row in applied if principal_of(row) == key]
    
            def carry_of(field):
                return [
                    row
                    for row in carried
                    if principal_of(row) == key and row["carry"] == field
                ]
    
            after_delta = int(base["max_records_base"])
            after_delta += sum(int(r["max_records_delta"]) for r in mine)
            after_delta += sum(
                int(r["max_records_delta"]) for r in 
carry_of("max_records_delta")
            )
            max_records = min(cap, round_half_up_bps(after_delta, mult_bps))
    
            retry_limit = int(base["retry_limit_base"])
            retry_limit += sum(int(r["retry_limit_delta"]) for r in mine)
    
            timeout_ms = int(base["timeout_seconds_base"]) * ms_factor
            for row in mine:
                timeout_ms += added_ms[int(row["seq"])]
                timeout_ms = max(timeout_ms, timeout_floor)
    
            terms = [int(base["risk_weight_base"])]
            terms += [int(r["risk_delta"]) for r in mine]
            terms += [int(r["risk_delta"]) for r in carry_of("risk_delta")]
            contribution = sum(round_half_up_bps(term, mult_bps) for term in 
terms)
    
            return {
                "max_records": max_records,
                "retry_limit": retry_limit,
                "timeout_ms": timeout_ms,
                "contribution": contribution,
                "base_threshold": tier_curve[level],
                "applied": mine,
            }
    
        # the iteration divisor is the one under which every standing curve 
report converges
        last_reset = max(
            (int(r["seq"]) for r in reports if r.get("kind") == "reset"), 
default=None
        )
        standing = [
            r
            for r in reports
            if r.get("kind") == "curve" and (last_reset is None or int(r["seq"])
> last_reset)
        ]
        if standing:
            candidates = []
            for candidate in divisor_range:
                for report in standing:
                    rkey, rtenant, rlevel = role_params(report["role"])
                    rparams = compile_params(rkey, rtenant, rlevel)
                    _, got = _run_curve(
                        rparams["base_threshold"],
                        rparams["contribution"],
                        candidate,
                        epsilon,
                        max_iterations,
                    )
                    if got != int(report["converged"]):
                        break
                else:
                    candidates.append(candidate)
            divisor = candidates[0] if len(candidates) == 1 else None
        else:
            divisor = int(rec["iteration_divisor"])
    
        params = compile_params(param_key, tenant, role_level)
        if divisor is None:
            iterations, converged = [params["base_threshold"]], None
        else:
            iterations, converged = _run_curve(
                params["base_threshold"],
                params["contribution"],
                divisor,
                epsilon,
                max_iterations,
            )
    
        schema = load_json(cx.path("config_schema.json"))
        defaults = load_json(cx.path("defaults.json"))
        resolved = {
            "profile": schema["profile"],
            "producer": schema["producer"],
            "container": schema["container"],
            "checksum": defaults["checksum"],
            "mode": defaults["mode"],
            "max_records": params["max_records"],
            "retry_limit": params["retry_limit"],
            "timeout_ms": params["timeout_ms"],
            "trailer": str(defaults["base_config"]["trailer"]),
            "param_key": param_key,
            "tenant": tenant,
            "effective_version": version,
            "risk_threshold": converged,
        }
    
        # controls are a parse check over every stored row, timeouts in 
milliseconds
        recomputed = {}
        for row in stored:
            entry = recomputed.setdefault(
                row["tenant"],
                {"max_records_delta_sum": 0, "rows": 0, 
"timeout_ms_delta_sum_ms": 0},
            )
            entry["rows"] += 1
            entry["max_records_delta_sum"] += int(row["max_records_delta"])
            entry["timeout_ms_delta_sum_ms"] += delta_ms(row)
        ledger_controls = {
            name: ("ok" if recomputed.get(name) == dict(control) else 
"mismatch")
            for name, control in controls.items()
        }
    
        protected = set()
        for row in stored:
            if int(row["seq"]) == latest_seq[row["override_id"]] and 
row["status"] == "reinstated":
                src = source_row(row)
                if src is not None:
                    protected.add(int(src["seq"]))
    
        def is_spare(row):
            if int(row["seq"]) in protected:
                return False
            if row["status"] == "withdrawn":
                return True
            if row["status"] == "superseded" and "carry" not in row:
                return True
            return int(row["seq"]) != latest_seq[row["override_id"]]
    
        ledger_repair = {}
        for name, state in ledger_controls.items():
            if state != "mismatch":
                continue
            got = recomputed.get(
                name, {"max_records_delta_sum": 0, "rows": 0, 
"timeout_ms_delta_sum_ms": 0}
            )
            control = controls[name]
            spares = [
                {"seq": int(r["seq"]), "mr": int(r["max_records_delta"]), "ms": 
delta_ms(r)}
                for r in stored
                if r["tenant"] == name and is_spare(r)
            ]
            ledger_repair[name] = _best_repair(
                spares,
                got["rows"] - int(control["rows"]),
                got["max_records_delta_sum"] - 
int(control["max_records_delta_sum"]),
                got["timeout_ms_delta_sum_ms"] - 
int(control["timeout_ms_delta_sum_ms"]),
            )
    
        rejected = []
    
        def reject(name, reason):
            rejected.append({"name": name, "reason": reason})
    
        reject(
            ignored_bundle,
            "bundle status " + str(ledger.get(ignored_bundle, {}).get("status"))
            + "; it is the " + stale["ignore"] + " and stale_version_rules.txt 
prefers "
            + stale["prefer"] + " " + active_bundle,
        )
        for bid in sorted(ledger):
            if bid not in (active_bundle, ignored_bundle) and 
ledger[bid].get("status") != "active":
                reject(bid, "ledger bundle status " + 
str(ledger[bid].get("status")))
        legacy_role = ledger.get(ignored_bundle, {}).get("role_key")
        if legacy_role:
            reject(legacy_role, "role_key of the superseded bundle " + 
ignored_bundle)
            if legacy_role + ".param_key" in index:
                reject(
                    index[legacy_role + ".param_key"],
                    "param_key of the superseded role " + legacy_role,
                )
        for name, why in (
            ("legacy_override_table.ini", "legacy override table of the 
superseded bundle"),
            ("superseded_windows.xml", "superseded effective-time windows"),
            ("principal_alias_table.jsonc", "conflicting aliases from the 
retired bundle"),
            ("legacy_policy_memo.pdf", "retired bundle memo, retained for audit 
only"),
            (
                "threshold_overrides.json",
                "tier deltas bound by no rule; the curve comes from 
threshold_tier_curve.parquet",
            ),
            (
                "tenant_region_multipliers.xlsx",
                "region multipliers; the tenant multiplier comes from 
tenant_multiplier_table.xlsx",
            ),
            ("effective_instant_examples.xml", "half-open window examples, 
context only"),
        ):
            if cx.has(name):
                reject(name, why)
        reject(
            "request_corpus.json",
            "client-claimed versions are not authoritative; the effective 
version "
            + version + " comes from policy_version_map.json",
        )
        for wid in sorted(windows):
            window = windows[wid]
            if int(window["start"]) <= instant < int(window["end"]):
                if window["version"] != version:
                    reject(wid, "window carries version " + window["version"] + 
", not " + version)
            elif int(window["end"]) == instant:
                reject(wid, "window span is half-open and ends at 
effective_instant")
        for row, reason in sorted(refused, key=lambda item: 
int(item[0]["seq"])):
            if principal_of(row) == param_key:
                reject(row["override_id"], reason)
    
        trace = {
            "active_bundle": active_bundle,
            "effective_version": version,
            "param_key": param_key,
            "tenant": tenant,
            "client_version_ignored": client_version_ignored,
            "applied_overrides": [row["override_id"] for row in 
params["applied"]],
            "ledger_controls": ledger_controls,
            "resolution_order": list(cx.order),
            "rejected_branches": rejected,
        }
        curve = {
            "tier": role_level,
            "base_threshold": params["base_threshold"],
            "contribution": params["contribution"],
            "iterations": iterations,
            "converged": converged,
        }
        return resolved, trace, curve, ledger_repair
    
    
    def cmd_init(args):
        schema = load_json(args.schema)
        data = {
            "profile": schema["profile"],
            "producer": schema["producer"],
            "container": schema["container"],
            "checksum": schema["checksum"],
            "mode": "strict-local",
        }
        write_json(args.out, data)
        print("initialized")
    
    
    def cmd_resolve(args):
        resolved, trace, curve, repair = resolve_policy(args.data_dir)
        out = Path(args.out)
        write_json(out / "policy_resolved.json", resolved)
        write_json(out / "dependency_trace.json", trace)
        write_json(out / "threshold_curve.json", curve)
        write_json(out / "ledger_repair.json", repair)
        print(json.dumps(resolved, sort_keys=True))
    
    
    def cmd_set(args):
        data = load_json(args.config)
        data[args.key] = coerce(args.value)
        assert_flat(data)
        write_json(args.config, data)
        print("set " + args.key)
    
    
    def cmd_validate(args):
        schema = load_json(args.schema)
        data = load_json(args.config)
        assert_flat(data)
        required = schema["required_keys"]
        if isinstance(required, str):
            required = [key.strip() for key in required.split(",") if 
key.strip()]
        missing = [key for key in required if key not in data]
        if missing:
            raise SystemExit("missing: " + ",".join(missing))
        print("ok")
    
    
    def cmd_report(args):
        data = load_json(args.config)
        report = {
            "ok": True,
            "tool": "sentinel-ctl",
            "commands": json.loads(args.commands),
            "config_sha256": args.digest,
            "trailer": data.get("trailer"),
        }
        write_json(args.out, report)
        print("reported")
    
    
    def main():
        parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(prog="sentinel-ctl")
        sub = parser.add_subparsers(dest="command")
    
        p = sub.add_parser("init")
        p.add_argument("--schema", required=True)
        p.add_argument("--out", required=True)
        p.set_defaults(func=cmd_init)
    
        p = sub.add_parser("resolve")
        p.add_argument("--data-dir", required=True)
        p.add_argument("--out", required=True)
        p.set_defaults(func=cmd_resolve)
    
        p = sub.add_parser("set")
        p.add_argument("--config", required=True)
        p.add_argument("--key", required=True)
        p.add_argument("--value", required=True)
        p.set_defaults(func=cmd_set)
    
        p = sub.add_parser("validate")
        p.add_argument("--schema", required=True)
        p.add_argument("--config", required=True)
        p.set_defaults(func=cmd_validate)
    
        p = sub.add_parser("export-report")
        p.add_argument("--config", required=True)
        p.add_argument("--out", required=True)
        p.add_argument("--digest", required=True)
        p.add_argument("--commands", required=True)
        p.set_defaults(func=cmd_report)
    
        args = parser.parse_args()
        if getattr(args, "func", None) is None:
            parser.error("a subcommand is required")
        args.func(args)
    
    
    if __name__ == "__main__":
        main()

+75|            continue
+76|        out[key.strip()] = value.strip()
+77|    return out
+78|
+79|
+80|def _parser(path):
+81|    parser = configparser.ConfigParser()
+82|    parser.read(path, encoding="utf-8")
+83|    return parser
+84|
+85|
+86|def read_conf(path):
+87|    parser = _parser(path)
+88|    flat = {}
+89|    for section in parser.sections():
+90|        for key, value in parser.items(section):
+91|            flat[key] = value
+92|    return flat
+93|
+94|
+95|def read_conf_section(path, section):
+96|    parser = _parser(path)
+97|    if not parser.has_section(section):
+98|        return {}
+99|    return dict(parser.items(section))
+100|
+101|
+102|def read_xml_records(path, tag):
+103|    root = ET.parse(path).getroot()
+104|    return [dict(node.attrib) for node in root.iter(tag)]
+105|
+106|
+107|def _col_to_idx(ref):
+108|    letters = re.match(r"[A-Z]+", ref).group(0)
+109|    idx = 0
+110|    for char in letters:
+111|        idx = idx * 26 + (ord(char) - ord("A") + 1)
+112|    return idx - 1
+113|
+114|
+115|def read_xlsx_rows(path):
+116|    ns = "{" + "http" + 
"://schemas.openxmlformats.org/spreadsheetml/2006/main}"
+117|    with zipfile.ZipFile(path) as zf:
+118|        sroot = ET.fromstring(zf.read("xl/worksheets/sheet1.xml"))
+119|        rows = []
+120|        for row in sroot.iter(ns + "row"):
+121|            cells = {}
+122|            for cell in row.iter(ns + "c"):
+123|                ref = cell.get("r", "A1")
+124|                col = _col_to_idx(ref)
+125|                vnode = cell.find(ns + "v")
+126|                if cell.get("t") == "inlineStr":
+127|                    tnode = cell.find(ns + "is/" + ns + "t")
+128|                    cells[col] = tnode.text if tnode is not None else ""
+129|                else:
+130|                    cells[col] = vnode.text if vnode is not None else ""
+131|            rows.append(cells)
+132|    header = [rows[0][i] for i in sorted(rows[0])]
+133|    records = []
+134|    for cells in rows[1:]:
+135|        records.append({name: cells.get(i, "") for i, name in 
enumerate(header)})
+136|    return records
+137|
+138|
+139|def read_parquet_rows(path):
+140|    import pyarrow.parquet as pq
+141|
+142|    return pq.read_table(path).to_pylist()
+143|
+144|
+145|def round_half_up_bps(value, bps):
+146|    """round_half_up(value * bps / 1000) in exact integer arithmetic."""
+147|    return (value * bps + 500) // 1000
+148|
+149|
+150|def _run_curve(base_threshold, contribution, divisor, epsilon, 
max_iterations):
+151|    iterations = [base_threshold]
+152|    current = base_threshold
+153|    for _ in range(max_iterations):
+154|        nxt = base_threshold + (contribution + current) // divisor
+155|        iterations.append(nxt)
+156|        if abs(nxt - current) <= epsilon:
+157|            return iterations, nxt
+158|        current = nxt
+159|    return iterations, None
+160|
+161|
+162|def _best_repair(spares, need_rows, need_mr, need_ms):
+163|    """Smallest total absolute timeout, ties to the first ascending seq 
list."""
+164|    if need_rows < 0 or need_rows > len(spares):
+165|        return None
+166|    rows = sorted(spares, key=lambda r: r["seq"])
+167|    best = None
+168|    for combo in itertools.combinations(rows, need_rows):
+169|        if sum(r["mr"] for r in combo) != need_mr:
+170|            continue
+171|        if sum(r["ms"] for r in combo) != need_ms:
+172|            continue
+173|        seqs = [r["seq"] for r in combo]
+174|        key = (sum(abs(r["ms"]) for r in combo), seqs)
+175|        if best is None or key < best[0]:
+176|            best = (key, seqs)
+177|    return best[1] if best else None
+178|
+179|
+180|class Corpus:
+181|    """The reference material, recording every file in the order it is 
consumed."""
+182|
+183|    def __init__(self, data_dir):
+184|        self.dir = Path(data_dir)
+185|        self.order = []
+186|
+187|    def path(self, name):
+188|        if name not in self.order:
+189|            self.order.append(name)
+190|        return self.dir / name
+191|
+192|    def text(self, name):
+193|        return Path(self.path(name)).read_text(encoding="utf-8")
+194|
+195|    def has(self, name):
+196|        return (self.dir / name).exists()
+197|
+198|
+199|def resolve_policy(data_dir):
+200|    cx = Corpus(data_dir)
+201|
+202|    # boot manifest -> stale version rules -> ledger -> principal index -> 
overrides
+203|    manifest = read_toml(cx.path("boot_manifest.toml"))["manifest"]
+204|    instant = int(manifest["effective_instant"])
+205|    stale = read_kv_text(cx.path("stale_version_rules.txt"))
+206|    active_bundle = str(manifest[stale["prefer"]])
+207|    ignored_bundle = str(manifest[stale["ignore"]])
+208|
+209|    ledger = read_jsonc(cx.path(manifest["ledger_ref"]))
+210|    bundle = ledger[active_bundle]
+211|    role_key = bundle["role_key"]
+212|    index = read_yaml_flat(cx.path(bundle["index_file"]))
+213|
+214|    def role_params(key):
+215|        return (
+216|            index[key + ".param_key"],
+217|            index[key + ".tenant"],
+218|            int(index[key + ".role_level"]),
+219|        )
+220|
+221|    param_key, tenant, role_level = role_params(role_key)
+222|
+223|    vmap = load_json(cx.path("policy_version_map.json"))
+224|    version_rows = vmap["versions"]
+225|    version = max(
+226|        (v for v in version_rows if int(v["effective_from"]) <= instant),
+227|        key=lambda v: int(v["effective_from"]),
+228|    )["version"]
+229|    effective_from = {v["version"]: int(v["effective_from"]) for v in 
version_rows}
+230|
+231|    corpus_doc = load_json(cx.path("request_corpus.json"))
+232|    client_version_ignored = any(
+233|        entry["claimed_version"] != version for entry in 
corpus_doc["entries"]
+234|    )
+235|
+236|    units_text = cx.text("units_reference.txt")
+237|    cutover = int(re.search(r"cutover:\s*(-?\d+)", units_text).group(1))
+238|    ms_factor = int(re.search(r"ms\s*=\s*seconds\s*\*\s*(\d+)", 
units_text).group(1))
+239|    timeout_floor = int(re.search(r"raised to (\d+)", units_text).group(1))
+240|
+241|    base_rows = {
+242|        row["param_key"]: row
+243|        for row in read_parquet_rows(cx.path("base_parameters.parquet"))
+244|    }
+245|
+246|    odoc = load_json(cx.path(index["overrides_ref"]))
+247|    stored = sorted(odoc["overrides"], key=lambda r: int(r["seq"]))
+248|    alias_map = {a["alias"]: a for a in odoc.get("param_key_aliases", [])}
+249|    reports = odoc.get("reports", [])
+250|    controls = odoc.get("controls", {})
+251|
+252|    windows = {
+253|        w["id"]: w for w in 
read_xml_records(cx.path("effective_windows.xml"), "window")
+254|    }
+255|    read_conf(cx.path("override_precedence.conf"))
+256|    catalog = {
+257|        t["id"]: t for t in read_xml_records(cx.path("tenant_catalog.xml"),
"tenant")
+258|    }
+259|    allowance = {
+260|        key: int(value)
+261|        for key, value in read_conf_section(
+262|            cx.path("quota_limits.cfg"), "override_allowance"
+263|        ).items()
+264|    }
+265|
+266|    def principal_of(row):
+267|        entry = alias_map.get(row["param_key"])
+268|        if entry is None:
+269|            return row["param_key"]
+270|        if int(entry["start"]) <= instant < int(entry["end"]):
+271|            return entry["principal"]
+272|        return None
+273|
+274|    def delta_ms(row):
+275|        value = int(row["timeout_ms_delta"])
+276|        return value * ms_factor if effective_from[row["version"]] < 
cutover else value
+277|
+278|    latest_seq = {}
+279|    for row in stored:
+280|        latest_seq[row["override_id"]] = int(row["seq"])
+281|
+282|    def source_row(row):
+283|        """A reinstated row adds the deltas of the last active row below 
its seq."""
+284|        if row["status"] != "reinstated":
+285|            return row
+286|        prior = [
+287|            r
+288|            for r in stored
+289|            if r["override_id"] == row["override_id"]
+290|            and r["status"] == "active"
+291|            and int(r["seq"]) < int(row["seq"])
+292|        ]
+293|        return prior[-1] if prior else None
+294|
+295|    # selection per override_precedence.conf
+296|    refused = []
+297|    passing = []
+298|    for row in stored:
+299|        window = windows.get(row["window_id"])
+300|        if int(row["seq"]) != latest_seq[row["override_id"]]:
+301|            reason = "reissued: the same override_id is stored at a higher 
seq"
+302|        elif row["status"] not in SELECTABLE_STATUS:
+303|            reason = "status " + row["status"] + " passes no selection"
+304|        elif window is None:
+305|            reason = "window " + row["window_id"] + " is not in 
effective_windows.xml"
+306|        elif not int(window["start"]) <= instant < int(window["end"]):
+307|            reason = (
+308|                "window " + row["window_id"]
+309|                + " does not contain effective_instant in its half-open 
span"
+310|            )
+311|        elif row["version"] != version:
+312|            reason = (
+313|                "row version " + row["version"]
+314|                + " is not the effective version " + version
+315|            )
+316|        elif window["version"] != version:
+317|            reason = (
+318|                "window " + row["window_id"] + " carries version " + 
window["version"]
+319|                + ", not the effective version " + version
+320|            )
+321|        elif source_row(row) is None:
+322|            reason = "reinstated with no earlier active row of its 
override_id"
+323|        else:
+324|            reason = None
+325|        (passing if reason is None else refused).append(
+326|            row if reason is None else (row, reason)
+327|        )
+328|
+329|    # draw: ascending seq, one region allowance unit and one window admit 
unit each
+330|    admit_left = {wid: int(w.get("admit", 0)) for wid, w in 
windows.items()}
+331|    allow_left = dict(allowance)
+332|    applied = []
+333|    applied_ids = set()
+334|    for row in passing:
+335|        region = catalog[row["tenant"]]["region"]
+336|        if "requires" in row and row["requires"] not in applied_ids:
+337|            refused.append(
+338|                (
+339|                    row,
+340|                    "requires " + str(row["requires"])
+341|                    + ", which was not applied at a lower seq",
+342|                )
+343|            )
+344|            continue
+345|        if allow_left.get(region, 0) <= 0:
+346|            refused.append(
+347|                (row, "region " + region + " has no override_allowance unit
left")
+348|            )
+349|            continue
+350|        if admit_left.get(row["window_id"], 0) <= 0:
+351|            refused.append((row, "window " + row["window_id"] + " has no 
admit unit left"))
+352|            continue
+353|        allow_left[region] -= 1
+354|        admit_left[row["window_id"]] -= 1
+355|        applied.append(row)
+356|        applied_ids.add(row["override_id"])
+357|
+358|    # the applied rows of a window share that window's timeout_pool
+359|    added_ms = {}
+360|    for wid in sorted({row["window_id"] for row in applied}):
+361|        pool = int(windows[wid].get("timeout_pool", 0))
+362|        positive = []
+363|        for row in applied:
+364|            if row["window_id"] != wid:
+365|                continue
+366|            value = delta_ms(source_row(row))
+367|            if value > 0:
+368|                positive.append((int(row["seq"]), value))
+369|            else:
+370|                added_ms[int(row["seq"])] = value
+371|        total = sum(value for _, value in positive)
+372|        if total <= pool:
+373|            for seq, value in positive:
+374|                added_ms[seq] = value
+375|            continue
+376|        shares = {seq: pool * value // total for seq, value in positive}
+377|        keep = [(seq, value) for seq, value in positive if shares[seq] >= 
100]
+378|        kept = sum(value for _, value in keep)
+379|        for seq, _ in positive:
+380|            added_ms[seq] = 0
+381|        for seq, value in keep:
+382|            added_ms[seq] = pool * value // kept
+383|
+384|    # a superseded row with a carry field contributes that one delta
+385|    carried = []
+386|    for row in stored:
+387|        window = windows.get(row["window_id"])
+388|        if row["status"] != "superseded" or "carry" not in row or window is
None:
+389|            continue
+390|        if not int(window["start"]) <= instant < int(window["end"]):
+391|            continue
+392|        if row["version"] == version and window["version"] == version:
+393|            carried.append(row)
+394|
+395|    multipliers = {
+396|        row["tenant"]: row
+397|        for row in read_xlsx_rows(cx.path("tenant_multiplier_table.xlsx"))
+398|    }
+399|    cx.text("clamp_rules.txt")
+400|    cx.text("risk_weight_rounding.txt")
+401|    tier_curve = {
+402|        int(row["tier"]): int(row["base_threshold"])
+403|        for row in 
read_parquet_rows(cx.path("threshold_tier_curve.parquet"))
+404|    }
+405|
+406|    conv_text = cx.text("convergence_spec.txt")
+407|    epsilon = int(re.search(r"convergence_epsilon\s*=\s*(-?\d+)", 
conv_text).group(1))
+408|    max_iterations = int(re.search(r"max_iterations\s*=\s*(\d+)", 
conv_text).group(1))
+409|
+410|    rec_text = cx.text("reconciliation_rules.conf")
+411|    rec = read_conf(cx.path("reconciliation_rules.conf"))
+412|    bounds = re.search(r"whole number from (\d+) to (\d+)", rec_text)
+413|    divisor_range = range(int(bounds.group(1)), int(bounds.group(2)) + 1)
+414|
+415|    def compile_params(key, tenant_id, level):
+416|        base = base_rows[key]
+417|        mult = multipliers[tenant_id]
+418|        mult_bps = int(mult["mult_bps"])
+419|        cap = int(mult["max_records_cap"])
+420|        mine = [row for row in applied if principal_of(row) == key]
+421|
+422|        def carry_of(field):
+423|            return [
+424|                row
+425|                for row in carried
+426|                if principal_of(row) == key and row["carry"] == field
+427|            ]
+428|
+429|        after_delta = int(base["max_records_base"])
+430|        after_delta += sum(int(r["max_records_delta"]) for r in mine)
+431|        after_delta += sum(
+432|            int(r["max_records_delta"]) for r in 
carry_of("max_records_delta")
+433|        )
+434|        max_records = min(cap, round_half_up_bps(after_delta, mult_bps))
+435|
+436|        retry_limit = int(base["retry_limit_base"])
+437|        retry_limit += sum(int(r["retry_limit_delta"]) for r in mine)
+438|
+439|        timeout_ms = int(base["timeout_seconds_base"]) * ms_factor
+440|        for row in mine:
+441|            timeout_ms += added_ms[int(row["seq"])]
+442|            timeout_ms = max(timeout_ms, timeout_floor)
+443|
+444|        terms = [int(base["risk_weight_base"])]
+445|        terms += [int(r["risk_delta"]) for r in mine]
+446|        terms += [int(r["risk_delta"]) for r in carry_of("risk_delta")]
+447|        contribution = sum(round_half_up_bps(term, mult_bps) for term in 
terms)
+448|
+449|        return {
+450|            "max_records": max_records,
+451|            "retry_limit": retry_limit,
+452|            "timeout_ms": timeout_ms,
+453|            "contribution": contribution,
+454|            "base_threshold": tier_curve[level],
+455|            "applied": mine,
+456|        }
+457|
+458|    # the iteration divisor is the one under which every standing curve 
report converges
+459|    last_reset = max(
+460|        (int(r["seq"]) for r in reports if r.get("kind") == "reset"), 
default=None
+461|    )
+462|    standing = [
+463|        r
+464|        for r in reports
+465|        if r.get("kind") == "curve" and (last_reset is None or 
int(r["seq"]) > last_reset)
+466|    ]
+467|    if standing:
+468|        candidates = []
+469|        for candidate in divisor_range:
+470|            for report in standing:
+471|                rkey, rtenant, rlevel = role_params(report["role"])
+472|                rparams = compile_params(rkey, rtenant, rlevel)
+473|                _, got = _run_curve(
+474|                    rparams["base_threshold"],
+475|                    rparams["contribution"],
+476|                    candidate,
+477|                    epsilon,
+478|                    max_iterations,
+479|                )
+480|                if got != int(report["converged"]):
+481|                    break
+482|            else:
+483|                candidates.append(candidate)
+484|        divisor = candidates[0] if len(candidates) == 1 else None
+485|    else:
+486|        divisor = int(rec["iteration_divisor"])
+487|
+488|    params = compile_params(param_key, tenant, role_level)
+489|    if divisor is None:
+490|        iterations, converged = [params["base_threshold"]], None
+491|    else:
+492|        iterations, converged = _run_curve(
+493|            params["base_threshold"],
+494|            params["contribution"],
+495|            divisor,
+496|            epsilon,
+497|            max_iterations,
+498|        )
+499|
+500|    schema = load_json(cx.path("config_schema.json"))
+501|    defaults = load_json(cx.path("defaults.json"))
+502|    resolved = {
+503|        "profile": schema["profile"],
+504|        "producer": schema["producer"],
+505|        "container": schema["container"],
+506|        "checksum": defaults["checksum"],
+507|        "mode": defaults["mode"],
+508|        "max_records": params["max_records"],
+509|        "retry_limit": params["retry_limit"],
+510|        "timeout_ms": params["timeout_ms"],
+511|        "trailer": str(defaults["base_config"]["trailer"]),
+512|        "param_key": param_key,
+513|        "tenant": tenant,
+514|        "effective_version": version,
+515|        "risk_threshold": converged,
+516|    }
+517|
+518|    # controls are a parse check over every stored row, timeouts in 
milliseconds
+519|    recomputed = {}
+520|    for row in stored:
+521|        entry = recomputed.setdefault(
+522|            row["tenant"],
+523|            {"max_records_delta_sum": 0, "rows": 0, 
"timeout_ms_delta_sum_ms": 0},
+524|        )
+525|        entry["rows"] += 1
+526|        entry["max_records_delta_sum"] += int(row["max_records_delta"])
+527|        entry["timeout_ms_delta_sum_ms"] += delta_ms(row)
+528|    ledger_controls = {
+529|        name: ("ok" if recomputed.get(name) == dict(control) else 
"mismatch")
+530|        for name, control in controls.items()
+531|    }
+532|
+533|    protected = set()
+534|    for row in stored:
+535|        if int(row["seq"]) == latest_seq[row["override_id"]] and 
row["status"] == "reinstated":
+536|            src = source_row(row)
+537|            if src is not None:
+538|                protected.add(int(src["seq"]))
+539|
+540|    def is_spare(row):
+541|        if int(row["seq"]) in protected:
+542|            return False
+543|        if row["status"] == "withdrawn":
+544|            return True
+545|        if row["status"] == "superseded" and "carry" not in row:
+546|            return True
+547|        return int(row["seq"]) != latest_seq[row["override_id"]]
+548|
+549|    ledger_repair = {}
+550|    for name, state in ledger_controls.items():
+551|        if state != "mismatch":
+552|            continue
+553|        got = recomputed.get(
+554|            name, {"max_records_delta_sum": 0, "rows": 0, 
"timeout_ms_delta_sum_ms": 0}
+555|        )
+556|        control = controls[name]
+557|        spares = [
+558|            {"seq": int(r["seq"]), "mr": int(r["max_records_delta"]), "ms":
delta_ms(r)}
+559|            for r in stored
+560|            if r["tenant"] == name and is_spare(r)
+561|        ]
+562|        ledger_repair[name] = _best_repair(
+563|            spares,
+564|            got["rows"] - int(control["rows"]),
+565|            got["max_records_delta_sum"] - 
int(control["max_records_delta_sum"]),
+566|            got["timeout_ms_delta_sum_ms"] - 
int(control["timeout_ms_delta_sum_ms"]),
+567|        )
+568|
+569|    rejected = []
+570|
+571|    def reject(name, reason):
+572|        rejected.append({"name": name, "reason": reason})
+573|
+574|    reject(
+575|        ignored_bundle,
+576|        "bundle status " + str(ledger.get(ignored_bundle, 
{}).get("status"))
+577|        + "; it is the " + stale["ignore"] + " and stale_version_rules.txt 
prefers "
+578|        + stale["prefer"] + " " + active_bundle,
+579|    )
+580|    for bid in sorted(ledger):
+581|        if bid not in (active_bundle, ignored_bundle) and 
ledger[bid].get("status") != "active":
+582|            reject(bid, "ledger bundle status " + 
str(ledger[bid].get("status")))
+583|    legacy_role = ledger.get(ignored_bundle, {}).get("role_key")
+584|    if legacy_role:
+585|        reject(legacy_role, "role_key of the superseded bundle " + 
ignored_bundle)
+586|        if legacy_role + ".param_key" in index:
+587|            reject(
+588|                index[legacy_role + ".param_key"],
+589|                "param_key of the superseded role " + legacy_role,
+590|            )
+591|    for name, why in (
+592|        ("legacy_override_table.ini", "legacy override table of the 
superseded bundle"),
+593|        ("superseded_windows.xml", "superseded effective-time windows"),
+594|        ("principal_alias_table.jsonc", "conflicting aliases from the 
retired bundle"),
+595|        ("legacy_policy_memo.pdf", "retired bundle memo, retained for audit
only"),
+596|        (
+597|            "threshold_overrides.json",
+598|            "tier deltas bound by no rule; the curve comes from 
threshold_tier_curve.parquet",
+599|        ),
+600|        (
+601|            "tenant_region_multipliers.xlsx",
+602|            "region multipliers; the tenant multiplier comes from 
tenant_multiplier_table.xlsx",
+603|        ),
+604|        ("effective_instant_examples.xml", "half-open window examples, 
context only"),
+605|    ):
+606|        if cx.has(name):
+607|            reject(name, why)
+608|    reject(
+609|        "request_corpus.json",
+610|        "client-claimed versions are not authoritative; the effective 
version "
+611|        + version + " comes from policy_version_map.json",
+612|    )
+613|    for wid in sorted(windows):
+614|        window = windows[wid]
+615|        if int(window["start"]) <= instant < int(window["end"]):
+616|            if window["version"] != version:
+617|                reject(wid, "window carries version " + window["version"] +
", not " + version)
+618|        elif int(window["end"]) == instant:
+619|            reject(wid, "window span is half-open and ends at 
effective_instant")
+620|    for row, reason in sorted(refused, key=lambda item: 
int(item[0]["seq"])):
+621|        if principal_of(row) == param_key:
+622|            reject(row["override_id"], reason)
+623|
+624|    trace = {
+625|        "active_bundle": active_bundle,
+626|        "effective_version": version,
+627|        "param_key": param_key,
+628|        "tenant": tenant,
+629|        "client_version_ignored": client_version_ignored,
+630|        "applied_overrides": [row["override_id"] for row in 
params["applied"]],
+631|        "ledger_controls": ledger_controls,
+632|        "resolution_order": list(cx.order),
+633|        "rejected_branches": rejected,
+634|    }
+635|    curve = {
+636|        "tier": role_level,
+637|        "base_threshold": params["base_threshold"],
+638|        "contribution": params["contribution"],
+639|        "iterations": iterations,
+640|        "converged": converged,
+641|    }
+642|    return resolved, trace, curve, ledger_repair
+643|
+644|
+645|def cmd_init(args):
+646|    schema = load_json(args.schema)
+647|    data = {
+648|        "profile": schema["profile"],
+649|        "producer": schema["producer"],
+650|        "container": schema["container"],
+651|        "checksum": schema["checksum"],
+652|        "mode": "strict-local",
+653|    }
+654|    write_json(args.out, data)
+655|    print("initialized")
+656|
+657|
+658|def cmd_resolve(args):
+659|    resolved, trace, curve, repair = resolve_policy(args.data_dir)
+660|    out = Path(args.out)
+661|    write_json(out / "policy_resolved.json", resolved)
+662|    write_json(out / "dependency_trace.json", trace)
+663|    write_json(out / "threshold_curve.json", curve)
+664|    write_json(out / "ledger_repair.json", repair)
+665|    print(json.dumps(resolved, sort_keys=True))
+666|
+667|
+668|def cmd_set(args):
+669|    data = load_json(args.config)
+670|    data[args.key] = coerce(args.value)
+671|    assert_flat(data)
+672|    write_json(args.config, data)
+673|    print("set " + args.key)
+674|
+675|
+676|def cmd_validate(args):
+677|    schema = load_json(args.schema)
+678|    data = load_json(args.config)
+679|    assert_flat(data)
+680|    required = schema["required_keys"]
+681|    if isinstance(required, str):
+682|        required = [key.strip() for key in required.split(",") if 
key.strip()]
+683|    missing = [key for key in required if key not in data]
+684|    if missing:
+685|        raise SystemExit("missing: " + ",".join(missing))
+686|    print("ok")
+687|
+688|
+689|def cmd_report(args):
+690|    data = load_json(args.config)
+691|    report = {
+692|        "ok": True,
+693|        "tool": "sentinel-ctl",
+694|        "commands": json.loads(args.commands),
+695|        "config_sha256": args.digest,
+696|        "trailer": data.get("trailer"),
+697|    }
+698|    write_json(args.out, report)
+699|    print("reported")
+700|
+701|
+702|def main():
+703|    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(prog="sentinel-ctl")
+704|    sub = parser.add_subparsers(dest="command")
+705|
+706|    p = sub.add_parser("init")
+707|    p.add_argument("--schema", required=True)
+708|    p.add_argument("--out", required=True)
+709|    p.set_defaults(func=cmd_init)
+710|
+711|    p = sub.add_parser("resolve")
+712|    p.add_argument("--data-dir", required=True)
+713|    p.add_argument("--out", required=True)
+714|    p.set_defaults(func=cmd_resolve)
+715|
+716|    p = sub.add_parser("set")
+717|    p.add_argument("--config", required=True)
+718|    p.add_argument("--key", required=True)
+719|    p.add_argument("--value", required=True)
+720|    p.set_defaults(func=cmd_set)
+721|
+722|    p = sub.add_parser("validate")
+723|    p.add_argument("--schema", required=True)
+724|    p.add_argument("--config", required=True)
+725|    p.set_defaults(func=cmd_validate)
+726|
+727|    p = sub.add_parser("export-report")
+728|    p.add_argument("--config", required=True)
+729|    p.add_argument("--out", required=True)
+730|    p.add_argument("--digest", required=True)
+731|    p.add_argument("--commands", required=True)
+732|    p.set_defaults(func=cmd_report)
+733|
+734|    args = parser.parse_args()
+735|    if getattr(args, "func", None) is None:
+736|        parser.error("a subcommand is required")
+737|    args.func(args)
+738|
+739|
+740|if __name__ == "__main__":
+741|    main()
+742|

Reasoning:
My calculation checks out, with max_records correctly resolving to 285 matching 
the authoritative cap. Now I need to verify whether risk_threshold belongs in 
config.json based on how policy_resolved is defined in the spec.

Confirming that config has 9 keys while policy_resolved has 13, matching what I 
resolved. Now I'm planning the orchestrator to run the actual CLI commands: 
starting with the resolve step writing its output files into /app/out so the 
trace artifacts are available alongside the seven required artifacts, then 
moving on to the init step that writes the config profile using the schema file.

I'm then setting the specific field values (max_records, retry_limit, 
timeout_ms, trailer) before validating the config against the schema, and 
repeating a similar init/set sequence for the dev profile, confirming it ends up
with the required 9 keys. After that, I need to run export-report with the 
correct digest and command transcript, then write the validation and summary 
reports in the delivery order specified by validation_rules.json.

For tool.zip, I'm reconstructing the original deterministic zip by sorting 
members, using ZIP_STORED compression, setting the fixed 1980-01-01 timestamp, 
0644 file mode, and matching the unix create_system value that Python's zipfile 
defaults to on non-Windows platforms.

Now I need to compute the sha256 hash of the config bytes after all fields are 
set, build the commands transcript capturing argv/returncode/stdout, and 
structure validation_report.json with a nested "checks" object holding the 
dotted-key entries alongside top-level "ok" and "policy_resolved" fields.

I'm fleshing out the checks object: profile entries keyed by name with their 
trailer values, a required_artifacts list in delivery order, and an empty 
command_failures array. For inputs, I need to list every file in /app/data 
sorted (around 58 entries including tool.zip) as the input manifest, and set 
format_tag to "TASK" from the config schema profile — then write build.py while 
making sure no forbidden keys like timestamps sneak into the output.

Resolution matches my independent hand-computation. Now the build orchestrator:

Action: FileEditorAction

Arguments:
  command: "create"
  path: "/app/build.py"
  file_text: 
    """Drive the repaired sentinel-ctl CLI and assemble the delivered 
artifacts."""
    
    from __future__ import annotations
    
    import hashlib
    import json
    import subprocess
    import sys
    import zipfile
    from pathlib import Path
    
    DATA = Path("/app/data")
    TOOL = Path("/app/tool")
    OUT = Path("/app/out")
    CLI = [sys.executable, str(TOOL / "cli.py")]
    MEMBERS = ("README.md", "cli.py", "schema.json")
    DELIVERY = [
        "config.json",
        "report.json",
        "tool.zip",
        "profile_dev.json",
        "profile_prod.json",
        "validation_report.json",
        "summary.json",
    ]
    
    commands: list[dict] = []
    
    
    def run(*argv: str) -> str:
        proc = subprocess.run(CLI + list(argv), capture_output=True, text=True)
        commands.append(
            {
                "argv": ["sentinel-ctl"] + list(argv),
                "returncode": proc.returncode,
                "stdout": proc.stdout.strip(),
            }
        )
        if proc.returncode != 0:
            raise SystemExit("command failed: " + " ".join(argv) + "\n" + 
proc.stderr)
        return proc.stdout.strip()
    
    
    def write_json(path: Path, data) -> None:
        path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
        path.write_text(json.dumps(data, sort_keys=True, indent=2) + "\n", 
encoding="utf-8")
    
    
    def build_config(path: Path, values: dict) -> None:
        run("init", "--schema", str(TOOL / "schema.json"), "--out", str(path))
        for key in ("profile", "mode", "max_records", "retry_limit", 
"timeout_ms", "trailer"):
            run("set", "--config", str(path), "--key", key, "--value", 
str(values[key]))
        run("validate", "--schema", str(TOOL / "schema.json"), "--config", 
str(path))
    
    
    def build_archive(path: Path) -> None:
        with zipfile.ZipFile(path, "w", zipfile.ZIP_STORED) as zf:
            for name in sorted(MEMBERS):
                info = zipfile.ZipInfo(name, date_time=(1980, 1, 1, 0, 0, 0))
                info.external_attr = 0o100644 << 16
                info.compress_type = zipfile.ZIP_STORED
                zf.writestr(info, (TOOL / name).read_bytes())
    
    
    def main() -> None:
        OUT.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    
        run("resolve", "--data-dir", str(DATA), "--out", str(OUT))
        policy = json.loads((OUT / 
"policy_resolved.json").read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
        trace = json.loads((OUT / 
"dependency_trace.json").read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
        curve = json.loads((OUT / 
"threshold_curve.json").read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
        repair = json.loads((OUT / 
"ledger_repair.json").read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
    
        schema = json.loads((DATA / 
"config_schema.json").read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
        config_keys = list(schema["required_keys"])
        config_values = {key: policy[key] for key in config_keys}
        build_config(OUT / "config.json", config_values)
    
        env = json.loads((DATA / 
"env_overrides.json").read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
        format_tag = schema["profile"]
        trailer = policy["trailer"]
        profile_trailers = {}
        for name in json.loads((DATA / 
"validation_rules.json").read_text(encoding="utf-8"))["profiles"]:
            sidecar = json.loads(
                (DATA / ("sample_config_" + name + 
".json")).read_text(encoding="utf-8")
            )
            sde = env[name]
            profile_trailers[name] = trailer + ":" + name
            build_config(
                OUT / ("profile_" + name + ".json"),
                {
                    "profile": format_tag + "-" + name,
                    "mode": sde["mode"],
                    "max_records": policy["max_records"] + 
sidecar["max_records_delta"],
                    "retry_limit": policy["retry_limit"] + 
sde["retry_limit_delta"],
                    "timeout_ms": policy["timeout_ms"] + 
sde["timeout_ms_delta"],
                    "trailer": profile_trailers[name],
                },
            )
    
        build_archive(OUT / "tool.zip")
    
        digest = hashlib.sha256((OUT / "config.json").read_bytes()).hexdigest()
        run(
            "export-report",
            "--config",
            str(OUT / "config.json"),
            "--out",
            str(OUT / "report.json"),
            "--digest",
            digest,
            "--commands",
            json.dumps(commands, sort_keys=True),
        )
    
        config = json.loads((OUT / "config.json").read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
        matches = all(config[key] == policy[key] for key in config_keys) and 
sorted(
            config
        ) == sorted(config_keys)
        scalar = all(not isinstance(value, (dict, list)) for value in 
config.values())
        present = [name for name in DELIVERY if name != "validation_report.json"
and name != "summary.json"]
        all_present = all((OUT / name).exists() for name in present)
        failures = [c["argv"] for c in commands if c["returncode"] != 0]
    
        write_json(
            OUT / "validation_report.json",
            {
                "ok": bool(matches and scalar and all_present and not failures),
                "checks": {
                    "all_artifacts_present": all_present,
                    "config_matches_policy": matches,
                    "config_scalar_flat": scalar,
                    "command_failures": failures,
                    "required_artifacts": DELIVERY,
                    "profiles": {
                        name: {"trailer": value} for name, value in 
profile_trailers.items()
                    },
                },
                "policy_resolved": policy,
                "threshold_curve": curve,
                "dependency_trace": trace,
                "ledger_repair": repair,
            },
        )
    
        inputs = sorted(p.name for p in DATA.iterdir() if p.is_file())
        write_json(
            OUT / "summary.json",
            {
                "artifact_count": len(DELIVERY),
                "artifacts": DELIVERY,
                "profiles": sorted(profile_trailers),
                "format_tag": format_tag,
                "trailer": trailer,
                "input_count": len(inputs),
                "inputs": inputs,
            },
        )
        print("built", len(commands), "commands")
    
    
    if __name__ == "__main__":
        main()
    

  kind: "FileEditorAction"

reasoning 1.78K (total 19.76K) • ↓ output 4.27K (total 48.77K) • $ (total 
2.4452)

$ cd /app/out && python -c "
import json,glob,re
bad=('timestamp','generated_at','uuid','random_seed')
def walk(o,p=''):
    if isinstance(o,dict):
        for k,v in o.items():
            if k in bad: print('FORBIDDEN KEY',p,k)
            walk(v,p+'/'+k)
    elif isinstance(o,list):
        for i,v in enumerate(o): walk(v,p+'[]')
for f in sorted(glob.glob('*.json')):
    t=open(f).read()
    walk(json.loads(t))
    for pat in (r'https?://', r'[a-z0-9.-]+\.(com|net|org|io|local)\b', 
r'(?i)(password|secret|token|api_key|private_key|BEGIN )'):
        m=re.findall(pat,t)
        if m: print(f,'MATCH',pat,set(m) if len(m)<10 else len(m))
print('scan done')
"

unresolved outcomes. Then d=12 converges to 36, and I'm partway through checking
d=13.

I confirm d=13 and d=14 both converge to 35, so the value 41 only arises 
uniquely from d=7, which identifies the divisor from the injected report with 
converged value 41. Now I'm applying that divisor to the actual principal's base
threshold of 40 and contribution of 63 to compute its own convergence.

Next I want to run several more checks: testing how a reset injection withdraws 
the standing report and changes the divisor, testing an ambiguous converged 
value that maps to multiple candidate divisors, testing carried risk deltas on a
superseded row, and testing requires/reinstated/alias/pool-overflow scenarios as
separate injections. I'm starting to script these test cases, and noting a 
subtlety about how compile_params uses the global applied state for a report's 
role.



Thought:
Let me verify the resolver's generality against synthetic corpus variants.

$ cd /tmp && cat > vtest.py <<'EOF'
import json, shutil, subprocess, sys
from pathlib import Path

SRC = Path("/app/data")
CLI = [sys.executable, "/app/tool/cli.py"]


def variant(name, mutate):
    d = Path("/tmp/v_" + name)
    if d.exists():
        shutil.rmtree(d)
    shutil.copytree(SRC, d)
    mutate(d)
    out = Path("/tmp/o_" + name)
    p = subprocess.run(CLI + ["resolve", "--data-dir", str(d), "--out", 
str(out)],
                       capture_output=True, text=True)
    if p.returncode:
        print(name, "FAILED", p.stderr[-1500:])
        return None
    return (json.loads((out / "policy_resolved.json").read_text()),
            json.loads((out / "threshold_curve.json").read_text()),
            json.loads((out / "dependency_trace.json").read_text()),
            json.loads((out / "ledger_repair.json").read_text()))


def edit_overrides(d, fn):
    doc = json.loads((d / "override_deltas.json").read_text())
    fn(doc)
    (d / "override_deltas.json").write_text(json.dumps(doc))


def check(label, got, want):
    print(("PASS " if got == want else "FAIL ") + label, "got", got, "want", 
want)


# 1. a standing curve report fixes the divisor at 7
r = variant("rep7", lambda d: edit_overrides(d, lambda doc: doc.__setitem__(
    "reports", [{"kind": "curve", "seq": 500, "role": "auditor", "converged": 
41}])))
check("report fixes divisor 7", (r[1]["converged"], r[1]["iterations"]), (57, 
[40, 54, 56, 57, 57]))

# 2. a later reset withdraws the report, so the conf divisor 10 applies
r = variant("reset", lambda d: edit_overrides(d, lambda doc: doc.__setitem__(
    "reports", [{"kind": "curve", "seq": 500, "role": "auditor", "converged": 
41},
                {"kind": "reset", "seq": 600}])))
check("reset -> conf divisor 10", r[1]["converged"], 51)

# 3. a report matching two divisors leaves the threshold unresolved
r = variant("ambig", lambda d: edit_overrides(d, lambda doc: doc.__setitem__(
    "reports", [{"kind": "curve", "seq": 500, "role": "auditor", "converged": 
37}])))
check("ambiguous divisor", (r[0]["risk_threshold"], r[1]["converged"], 
r[1]["iterations"]),
      (None, None, [40]))

# 4. a superseded carry row adds one delta without being applied
def add_carry(doc):
    doc["overrides"].append({"max_records_delta": 30, "override_id": 
"OV-K042-carry",
                             "param_key": "K042", "retry_limit_delta": 1, "rev":
2,
                             "risk_delta": 7, "seq": 300, "status": 
"superseded",
                             "tenant": "t-atlas", "timeout_ms_delta": 0, 
"version": "v100",
                             "window_id": "WIN-AUTH", "carry": "risk_delta"})
    doc["controls"]["t-atlas"] = {"max_records_delta_sum": 22, "rows": 6,
                                 "timeout_ms_delta_sum_ms": 2250}
r = variant("carry", lambda d: edit_overrides(d, add_carry))
# risk terms 57, 2, 7 -> 61 + 2 + 8 = 71 ; curve: 40 -> 51 -> 52 -> 52
check("carry risk_delta only", (r[1]["contribution"], r[1]["converged"],
                                r[0]["max_records"], r[2]["applied_overrides"]),
      (71, 52, 285, ["OV-K042-auth"]))
check("carry row is not spare", r[3]["t-atlas"], [3, 6])

# 5. requires: a row needing a later override is refused
def add_requires(doc):
    doc["overrides"].append({"max_records_delta": 10, "override_id": 
"OV-K042-req",
                             "param_key": "K042", "retry_limit_delta": 1, "rev":
2,
                             "risk_delta": 0, "seq": 4, "status": "active",
                             "tenant": "t-atlas", "timeout_ms_delta": 0, 
"version": "v100",
                             "window_id": "WIN-AUTH", "requires": 
"OV-K042-auth"})
r = variant("requires", lambda d: edit_overrides(d, add_requires))
check("requires at higher seq refused", r[2]["applied_overrides"], 
["OV-K042-auth"])

def add_requires_ok(doc):
    doc["overrides"].append({"max_records_delta": 10, "override_id": 
"OV-K042-req",
                             "param_key": "K042", "retry_limit_delta": 1, "rev":
2,
                             "risk_delta": 0, "seq": 7, "status": "active",
                             "tenant": "t-atlas", "timeout_ms_delta": 0, 
"version": "v100",
                             "window_id": "WIN-AUTH", "requires": 
"OV-K042-auth"})
r = variant("requires_ok", lambda d: edit_overrides(d, add_requires_ok))
check("requires at lower seq applied", r[2]["applied_overrides"],
      ["OV-K042-auth", "OV-K042-req"])

# 6. admit cap: WIN-AUTH admits 3; a fourth passing row is refused
def admit_cap(doc):
    for i, seq in enumerate((20, 21)):
        doc["overrides"].append({"max_records_delta": 0, "override_id": 
"OV-FILL-%d" % i,
                                 "param_key": "K042", "retry_limit_delta": 0, 
"rev": 2,
                                 "risk_delta": 0, "seq": seq, "status": 
"active",
                                 "tenant": "t-atlas", "timeout_ms_delta": 0,
                                 "version": "v100", "window_id": "WIN-AUTH"})
r = variant("admit", lambda d: edit_overrides(d, admit_cap))
check("window admit cap", r[2]["applied_overrides"], ["OV-K042-auth", 
"OV-FILL-0"])

# 7. region allowance cap: r-north allows 5
def allow_cap(doc):
    for i in range(6):
        doc["overrides"].append({"max_records_delta": 0, "override_id": "OV-A%d"
% i,
                                 "param_key": "K042", "retry_limit_delta": 0, 
"rev": 2,
                                 "risk_delta": 0, "seq": 30 + i, "status": 
"active",
                                 "tenant": "t-atlas", "timeout_ms_delta": 0,
                                 "version": "v100", "window_id": "WIN-AUTH"})
def allow_cap_dir(d):
    edit_overrides(d, allow_cap)
    text = (d / "effective_windows.xml").read_text().replace('admit="3"', 
'admit="9"', 1)
    (d / "effective_windows.xml").write_text(text)
r = variant("allow", allow_cap_dir)
check("region allowance cap", r[2]["applied_overrides"],
      ["OV-K042-auth", "OV-A0", "OV-A1", "OV-A2"])

# 8. reinstated rows take the deltas of the last active row
def reinstate(doc):
    rows = doc["overrides"]
    for extra in (
        {"max_records_delta": 7, "override_id": "OV-Z1", "param_key": "K042",
         "retry_limit_delta": 1, "rev": 2, "risk_delta": 1, "seq": 310, 
"status": "active",
         "tenant": "t-atlas", "timeout_ms_delta": 50, "version": "v100", 
"window_id": "WIN-AUTH"},
        {"max_records_delta": 9, "override_id": "OV-Z1", "param_key": "K042",
         "retry_limit_delta": 2, "rev": 2, "risk_delta": 0, "seq": 311, 
"status": "active",
         "tenant": "t-atlas", "timeout_ms_delta": 60, "version": "v100", 
"window_id": "WIN-AUTH"},
        {"max_records_delta": 0, "override_id": "OV-Z1", "param_key": "K042",
         "retry_limit_delta": 0, "rev": 2, "risk_delta": 0, "seq": 312, 
"status": "withdrawn",
         "tenant": "t-atlas", "timeout_ms_delta": 0, "version": "v100", 
"window_id": "WIN-AUTH"},
        {"max_records_delta": 2, "override_id": "OV-Z1", "param_key": "K042",
         "retry_limit_delta": 0, "rev": 2, "risk_delta": 0, "seq": 313, 
"status": "reinstated",
         "tenant": "t-atlas", "timeout_ms_delta": 0, "version": "v100", 
"window_id": "WIN-AUTH"},
    ):
        rows.append(extra)
r = variant("reinstated", lambda d: edit_overrides(d, reinstate))
# after_delta 300-24+9 = 285 -> round_half_up(285*1.075)=306 -> cap 285
check("reinstated takes last active deltas",
      (r[2]["applied_overrides"], r[0]["retry_limit"], r[0]["timeout_ms"]),
      (["OV-K042-auth", "OV-Z1"], 5, 2310))

# 9. param_key_aliases bind a row to another principal only inside the span
def alias(doc):
    doc["param_key_aliases"] = [{"alias": "K900", "principal": "K042",
                                 "start": 1699999000, "end": 1700001000}]
    doc["overrides"].append({"max_records_delta": 5, "override_id": "OV-ALIAS",
                             "param_key": "K900", "retry_limit_delta": 1, "rev":
2,
                             "risk_delta": 0, "seq": 320, "status": "active",
                             "tenant": "t-atlas", "timeout_ms_delta": 0, 
"version": "v100",
                             "window_id": "WIN-AUTH"})
r = variant("alias_in", lambda d: edit_overrides(d, alias))
check("alias inside span", (r[2]["applied_overrides"], r[0]["retry_limit"]),
      (["OV-K042-auth", "OV-ALIAS"], 4))

def alias_out(doc):
    alias(doc)
    doc["param_key_aliases"][0]["end"] = 1700000000
r = variant("alias_out", lambda d: edit_overrides(d, alias_out))
check("alias outside span draws but binds nothing",
      (r[2]["applied_overrides"], r[0]["retry_limit"]), (["OV-K042-auth"], 3))

# 10. timeout pool: summed positive deltas above the pool are shared pro rata
def pool(doc):
    doc["overrides"].append({"max_records_delta": 0, "override_id": "OV-P1",
                             "param_key": "K042", "retry_limit_delta": 0, "rev":
2,
                             "risk_delta": 0, "seq": 330, "status": "active",
                             "tenant": "t-atlas", "timeout_ms_delta": 900, 
"version": "v100",
                             "window_id": "WIN-AUTH"})
r = variant("pool", lambda d: edit_overrides(d, pool))
# positives 100, 250, 900 sum 1250 > pool 1200 -> shares 96, 240, 864
# 96 < 100 so the 100 ms row adds none; reshare over 250+900=1150: 260, 939
check("pool reshare", r[0]["timeout_ms"], 2000 + 260 + 939)

# 11. timeout floor and the seconds unit before the cutover
def floors(d):
    def fn(doc):
        rows = [r for r in doc["overrides"] if r["seq"] != 5]
        for seq, delta in ((5, -900), (340, -900), (341, -800), (342, 500)):
            rows.append({"max_records_delta": 0, "override_id": "OV-FL%d" % seq,
                         "param_key": "K042", "retry_limit_delta": 0, "rev": 2,
                         "risk_delta": 0, "seq": seq, "status": "active",
                         "tenant": "t-atlas", "timeout_ms_delta": delta, 
"version": "v100",
                         "window_id": "WIN-AUTH"})
        doc["overrides"] = rows
    edit_overrides(d, fn)
    text = (d / "effective_windows.xml").read_text().replace('admit="3"', 
'admit="9"', 1)
    (d / "effective_windows.xml").write_text(text)
    import pyarrow.parquet as pq, pyarrow as pa
    t = pq.read_table(d / "base_parameters.parquet").to_pylist()
    for row in t:
        if row["param_key"] == "K042":
            row["timeout_seconds_base"] = 3
    pq.write_table(pa.Table.from_pylist(t), d / "base_parameters.parquet")
r = variant("floor", floors)
check("timeout floor walk", r[0]["timeout_ms"], 1500)

Worst case looks like ~705k combinations at roughly 5 seconds, which is 
acceptable even with multiple such tenants. I'll set up a stress test by marking
all rows for one tenant as superseded spares and forcing removal of about half 
to confirm it completes well under the time limit.



$ cd /tmp && python - <<'EOF'
import json, shutil, subprocess, sys, time
from pathlib import Path
d=Path('/tmp/v_stress')
if d.exists(): shutil.rmtree(d)
shutil.copytree('/app/data', d)
doc=json.loads((d/'override_deltas.json').read_text())
vm={v['version']:v['effective_from'] for v in 
json.loads((d/'policy_version_map.json').read_text())['versions']}
def ms(r): return r['timeout_ms_delta']*1000 if vm[r['version']]<1650000000 else
r['timeout_ms_delta']
for t in ('t-f000','t-f001','t-f002','t-f003'):
    rows=[r for r in doc['overrides'] if r['tenant']==t]
    for r in rows: r['status']='superseded'; r.pop('carry',None)
    rows.sort(key=lambda r:r['seq'])
    drop=rows[1::2]
    doc['controls'][t]={'rows':len(rows)-len(drop),
        'max_records_delta_sum':sum(r['max_records_delta'] for r in 
rows)-sum(r['max_records_delta'] for r in drop),
        'timeout_ms_delta_sum_ms':sum(ms(r) for r in rows)-sum(ms(r) for r in 
drop)}
    print(t,'rows',len(rows),'drop',len(drop))
(d/'override_deltas.json').write_text(json.dumps(doc))
t0=time.time()
p=subprocess.run([sys.executable,'/app/tool/cli.py','resolve','--data-dir',str(d
),'--out','/tmp/o_stress'],capture_output=True,text=True)
print('rc',p.returncode,'elapsed %.1fs'%(time.time()-t0), p.stderr[-400:])
print(json.loads(Path('/tmp/o_stress/ledger_repair.json').read_text()))
EOF
