    # sentinel-ctl policy bundle task
    
    ## Layout
    - `/app/data` — read-only reference corpus (the binding policy).
    - `/app/tool` — extracted `sentinel-ctl` (`README.md`, `cli.py`, 
`schema.json`).
    - `/app/build.py` — orchestrator: drives the real CLI, assembles `/app/out`.
    - `/app/out` — delivered artifacts.
    
    ## Authoritative resolution chain (do not short-circuit)
    `boot_manifest.toml` → `stale_version_rules.txt` (prefer `active_bundle_id`,
    ignore `latest_bundle_id`) → `ledger_ref` (`bundle_ledger.jsonc`) → active
    bundle's `role_key` + `index_file` → `principal_role_index.yaml` → 
`overrides_ref`
    (`override_deltas.json`).
    
    ## Rules that bite
    - Every rule lives in the file holding the data it governs; files with no 
stated
      rule (`threshold_overrides.json`, `tenant_region_multipliers.xlsx`,
      `alias_table.json`, ...) are decoys.
    - Effective version comes from `policy_version_map.json`, never from the
      client-claimed version in `request_corpus.json`.
    - Windows are half-open `[start, end)`; row *and* window must carry the
      effective version.
    - `timeout_ms_delta` is stored in **whole seconds** when the row's version 
has an
      `effective_from` before the cutover in `units_reference.txt`, milliseconds
      otherwise. This matters for the `controls` sums too.
    - `max_records`: scale first with `round_half_up`, clamp to the cap after.
    - Risk contribution: `round_half_up` **per term**, then sum.
    - `base_threshold` is the curve row whose `tier == role_level` (no 
off-by-one).
    - Threshold reconciliation iterates to convergence; the divisor comes from 
the
      standing curve reports in `override_deltas.json`, else
      `reconciliation_rules.conf`.
    
    ## Resolved result for `/app/data`
    `B7` / `operator` / `K042` / `t-atlas` / `v100`, tier 4, contribution 63,
    iterations `[40, 50, 51, 51]`, `risk_threshold` 51, `max_records` 285,
    `retry_limit` 3, `timeout_ms` 2250, applied override `OV-K042-auth`,
    `ledger_repair` `{"t-atlas": [3, 6]}`.
    
    ## Python 3.12 notes
    `configparser.SafeConfigParser` was removed; use `ConfigParser`.

Message from Agent ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

All checks pass. Here's what was done.

## What was wrong

The tool failed to start on Python 3.12 (`configparser.SafeConfigParser`, 
removed in 3.12) and, once running, its `resolve` drifted from the authoritative
policy on eleven counts:

| Area | Defect | Authoritative rule (source file) |
|---|---|---|
| Dependency chain | read `latest_bundle_id` (`B9`, superseded) | 
`stale_version_rules.txt`: prefer `active_bundle_id` → `B7` → `operator` → 
`K042`/`t-atlas`/level 4 |
| Version | took the client's `claimed_version` from the corpus | 
`policy_version_map.json`: greatest `effective_from ≤ instant` → `v100` |
| Windows | `start <= t <= end`, no version check | `override_precedence.conf` +
`glossary.txt`: half-open `[start,end)`, row **and** window must carry `v100` |
| Selection | first match only, `active` only | reissued/withdrawn/reinstated 
handling, `admit` + region `override_allowance` draw in ascending `seq`, 
`requires` |
| Timeout unit | used `timeout_seconds_base` as if milliseconds | 
`units_reference.txt`: `ms = seconds * 1000`, pre-cutover deltas stored in 
seconds, 1000 ms running floor |
| Timeout pool | ignored | `override_precedence.conf` pro-rata pool sharing + 
100 ms re-share |
| `max_records` | clamped before scaling, floor rounding | `clamp_rules.txt`: 
`min(cap, round_half_up(after_delta·bps/1000))` |
| Risk | one floor over the product sum | `risk_weight_rounding.txt`: 
`round_half_up` **per term**, then sum |
| Tier | `tiers[role_level - 1]` | `convergence_spec.txt`: `tier == role_level` 
|
| Threshold | a single step | iterate to `convergence_epsilon`; divisor from 
standing curve reports, else `reconciliation_rules.conf` |
| Controls | summed raw deltas, empty trace, no repair | `override_deltas.json` 
conventions: ms-normalised sums, spare-row repair, trace/rejection records |

I also fixed `validate` (iterated a comma-separated string character by 
character), made JSON output deterministic (`sort_keys`, indent, trailing 
newline), and added the `ledger_repair.json` output.

## Resolved authoritative policy

`B7` → `operator` → `K042` / `t-atlas` / `v100`; applied override `OV-K042-auth`
only. `max_records` **285** (`min(285, round_half_up(276·1.075)=297)`), 
`retry_limit` **3**, `timeout_ms` **2250** (`2 s → 2000 ms`, `+250 ms` under 
pool 1200), tier 4 → base threshold 40, contribution 63 (`61+2`), iterations 
`[40, 50, 51, 51]`, `risk_threshold` **51**. `ledger_controls` flags `t-atlas` 
mismatch; its repair is spare rows `[3, 6]`.

## Verification

Beyond `/app/data`, I exercised 14 synthetic corpus variants covering standing 
curve reports fixing the divisor (7), report resets, ambiguous divisors 
(unresolved → nulls), `carry` rows, `requires`, admit/allowance caps, reinstated
deltas, `param_key_aliases` in and out of span, pool re-share, and the timeout 
floor walk — all pass. `resolve` runs in 0.13 s (1.2 s on a 4-tenant 
repair-search stress case), well inside 60 s. All artifacts are byte-identical 
across rebuilds and carry no timestamps, uuids, seeds, hosts, or secrets.
